## v101 (Oct 16, 2026) — Navigator DRIVE SCREEN + full-screen planning + three doors + Butterfly field fix
Owner's settled design, built as specified — no redesign:
- DRIVE SCREEN: a dedicated full-screen drive mode (fixed overlay), separate from the planning screen. Big next-turn instruction + distance-to-turn, current road name, remaining distance + ETA. Entry is ONE primary button "▶ Drive", revealed only when a server route is active (it takes the primary slot; Route demotes to a quiet ghost re-route). The v93 "▶ Turn-by-turn" handoff to the phone's maps app STAYS as an option.
- MAP RENDERING / 3D CHOICE: the app already ships Leaflet 1.9.4 (CDN) + OpenStreetMap tiles — the drive screen reuses exactly that stack (a SECOND Leaflet instance on its own container, created on Drive, removed on exit; same free OSM tile layer; no new library, no keys, $0). Leaflet has no native pitch, so the 3D view is CSS transforms wrapped around the map: a perspective viewport, a tilt wrapper, a spin wrapper rotating by −GPS-heading around the driver anchor (50% / 64%), and an oversized map box (210%) so the tilted/rotated plane never shows an edge. TILT VALUE: rotateX(52deg) — inside the 45–60° band of the standard navigation view the owner named (Google Maps, Rand McNally TND, CoPilot Truck, TomTom GO Expert, Garmin dēzl): horizon in the upper third, driver anchored in the lower portion (64% height), road ahead filling the view. Heading is null when stationary (platform reality) — the last real heading is kept so the map never spins at a stop.
- MANEUVER ENGINE: pure NAVPURE additions (currentRoadName / driveProgress / drivePrompt) over the maneuverPlan the Navigator already builds from the Valhalla response (maneuvers now also carry their street_names). Advance prompts: each upcoming maneuver is announced ONCE far out (600 m, its verbal pre-transition line) and ONCE close in (200 m, its instruction), spoken through the module's existing speak() and the ONE Sound toggle (the drive bar's mute button is a second face of the same settings.muted). The final maneuver gets no far prompt. Off-route: same >60 m distance-from-polyline threshold + time hysteresis as the planning tracker; while driving it shows the "Rerouting… / Off route" pill and the re-route lands on the drive map too (driveOnRoute resets prompt flags + redraws the line). Arrival paints "You have arrived", holds ~3 s, and exits drive mode back to the Navigator (route stays up until the driver ends it).
- GPS: drive mode adds NO new GPS consumer — it rides the Navigator module's one existing watchPosition stream (onPos now also passes coords.heading through). Coordinate with miles tracking unchanged (v92 armed-day machinery untouched).
- FULL-SCREEN PLANNING (owner addendum, Oct 16 ~16:02 MDT): the Navigator planning view is now a fixed full-screen shell (.nv-shell, z-index 60 — above the module page, below the app drawer/sheets): slim navy header with its own "← Back" (App.showDashboard), the map filling the screen between header and sheet, the status banner and the Truck/Car/Follow/Sound row overlaid ON the map, and the destination doors + Route/Turn-by-turn/Drive/End + Directions in a bottom sheet (max 47vh, scrolls internally). The small embedded map card is gone.
- THREE DOORS, COMPLETE (owner rule, Oct 16 16:02 MDT): (1) favorites/places dropdown, (2) typed address search (Nominatim, geocoding ONLY for text the user typed), (3) TAP-THE-MAP PIN: a single tap drops or moves the destination pin (Leaflet map click → pinTap → setDest); the destination line shows the point itself — "Dropped pin (lat, lng)" — and a best-effort reverse lookup on the SAME free Nominatim service adds the road/place name when it answers (no new service, no key; offline it stays coordinates). Route/Turn-by-turn consume that pin like any other destination.
- FIELD FIX — BUTTERFLY (field-found Oct 16, 2026 ~15:59 MDT, owner's screen recording): Navigation → favorite "Butterfly" → Navigate handed the Navigator a pending destination whose pin existed only as address text ("Butterfly, 50.9519460, -114.0273550" — the Navigation Add form has no pin field, so the pin was pasted into Address; goNavigate sent name + ', ' + address as the query), and the Navigator GEOCODED that text, offering "Butterfly Playground, Prestwick Heath SE" / "Butterfly Park, McKenzie Towne" — a children's playground kilometres from the real pin (50.9519460, -114.0273550). FIX: nav_pending consumption now resolves coordinates FIRST — explicit pend.lat/pend.lng, else parseLatLngText() over query/address/name (≥3 decimals required on both numbers + range-validated, so street text can never false-match) — and sets the destination from them directly; a coordinate-bearing arrival is NEVER geocoded. Only coordinate-free text falls through to the typed-search path. Harness-pinned: marker lands EXACTLY on 50.951946, -114.027355 with ZERO nominatim calls and the search box untouched.
- Copy honesty: the TTN note now reads exactly "Opens Google Maps for spoken directions." — the old "Meticulous's own drive screen is coming." is gone because the drive screen exists.
- Tests: navigator-test 84 → 126, all green (plan street names; currentRoadName; driveProgress at 0 m; drivePrompt far/near once-each + final-maneuver rules; parseLatLngText incl. the Butterfly string and street-text rejection; tap-pin coordinates on the destination line + marker exactness; Butterfly world; pend lat/lng world with zero fetches; drive flow world — reveal/demote, screen open, zoom-17 centre, instruction/distance/ETA paint, −heading spin, off-route-while-driving re-route + pill, arrival paint, End drive exit; shell/drive/tilt statics). ## Oct 16, 2026 (v102) — THE BRAIN GETS A SERVER; PLACES GET A REAL SEARCH
Owner's declared priority after navigation: "Today primarily you're supposed to be focusing on the chatbot I explained yesterday" — the v93/v94 seams (chatProxyUrl / powerReadProxyUrl) were built for this ship. Two halves:

- THE SERVER (new container `meticulous-ai` on the Navigator's Hetzner box, 2.31.47.19): a zero-dependency Node 20 service (`~/nav/ai/server.js` on the box; source in truck-nav/deploy/ai/) on the internal navnet only, proxied by the same Caddy at **POST /ai/chat** ({q} → {a} — Road Hand's general brain, prompt = the app's own layer-5 wording) and **POST /ai/powerread** ({reader, image} → {text} — the model's raw JSON text, exactly the shape js/powerread.js callProxy already parses; the reader prompts are the app's promptFor texts kept server-side). Model chain mirrors the app (Flash-class, 404 walks down, first answerer remembered). Caddy: both site blocks gained a path-scoped /ai/* preflight (Allow-Headers "Content-Type, X-Meticulous-App") placed BEFORE the generic preflight, and /ai/chat + /ai/powerread handles with the same header_down single-ACAO pin as /route; the generic preflight, /route and /status handling are byte-untouched; everything else still 404s. DOORS at the service: Origin must equal the app origin AND header X-Meticulous-App must match the server env token (a shared static turnstile — the same literal ships in the app bundle; it is abuse friction alongside CORS + rate limits, not a credential); per-IP limits (chat 30/min, powerread 12/min, via Caddy's X-Forwarded-For), body caps (16 KB chat / 12 MB read, decoded image ≤ 8 MB), max 4 concurrent model calls, JSON-only bodies.
- **KEY STATUS: NOT CONFIGURED — deliberately.** No Gemini key exists in the sandbox, on the box, or anywhere this build may legitimately take one from, and none was invented or fetched. `~/nav/ai.env` holds METICULOUS_APP_TOKEN (set) and GEMINI_API_KEY (EMPTY). With no key, both endpoints answer a clean **503 {"error":"brain_not_configured"}** after the door checks, and the app falls back to its honest lines (Road Hand's offline line; Power Read's plain failure line, photo + entries untouched). **THE ONE REMAINING STEP is the owner's: a free Google AI Studio key under his Google account goes into GEMINI_API_KEY in ~/nav/ai.env, then `docker compose up -d ai` — no app change, no redeploy of anything else.** The key lives ONLY in that env file: never in git, never in the app, never printed.
- SMOKE (from outside, through the public face): /status 200 with exactly one ACAO ✓ · POST /route (deploy test body) 200 with a real trip ✓ · OPTIONS /route 204 with the ORIGINAL preflight headers (Content-Type only) ✓ · OPTIONS /ai/chat 204, Allow-Headers "Content-Type, X-Meticulous-App", one ACAO ✓ · POST /ai/chat no token → 403, wrong token → 403, wrong Origin → 403 ✓ · good token + Origin → 503 brain_not_configured with exactly one ACAO (both endpoints) ✓ · GET /ai/chat → 405 · /locate → 404 ✓ · internal burst: 30 chat calls pass the limiter, the 31st+ are 429 ✓ · /ai/health (internal only) {"ok":true,"configured":false} ✓. Deploy gotcha worth keeping: the Caddyfile is a single-file bind mount — replacing the host file changes its inode and the running container keeps the OLD config; the fix was `docker compose up -d --force-recreate caddy` (a plain reload reads the stale mount). Backups: ~/nav/Caddyfile.bak-20261016-v102-ai, docker-compose.yml.bak-20261016-v102-ai. The local deploy package (~/workspace/truck-nav/deploy/) now matches live: ai/ service, ai.env.example, compose ai service, the full two-block Caddyfile (global default_sni + shortlived issuer + IP block) WITH the /ai routes, README section.
- THE APP (webVersion 102): (1) DEFAULTS SHIP — chatbot.js remoteAsk and powerread.js proxyUrl() resolve navigator-style: store key never saved → the shipped default (https://2.31.47.19/ai/chat, https://2.31.47.19/ai/powerread); a saved URL wins; a deliberately cleared '' stays cleared (reads stay on-device / general layer stays offline). Both proxy calls now send the X-Meticulous-App token header; the token literal is identical in js/chatbot.js and js/powerread.js (parity-pinned by test) and matches the server env. (2) SEARCH-FIRST PLACE PICKER (owner, Oct 16 15:57 MDT, screenshot of 48 favorites: the v87 <select> listbox was a cramped scroll-box, names truncated — "looking for one is a chore. There should be a search button"): the select is GONE from modules/navigation.js; the filter field is the primary element; matches render as full-width tappable rows (data-pid) with FULL names (white-space:normal — wrap, never truncate), type + Built-in/Yours + address on the meta line, ✓ on the picked row; ONE tap picks the place and arms the detail panel's Navigate (nav_pending hand-off untouched — the v101 Navigator, three doors and all, is not modified). Default order: most recently visited (lastVisit), then most visited (visits), then alphabetical — built-ins carry no visit data and sit in the alphabetical group. (3) index pins: js/chatbot.js, js/powerread.js, modules/navigation.js → ?v=102, plus modules/navigator.js → ?v=102 per the standing pin convention (its bytes did not change; card-previews/navigator suites pin it at the live webVersion).

## FIELD DEFECT — Oct 16, 2026 16:40 MDT (owner's screen recording): Turn-by-turn handoff dumps him on a Waze WEB page
- What he did: Navigation → Butterfly (opened correctly, pin 50.9519460,-114.0273550, "2 visits") → Navigate (Navigator armed "Destination: Butterfly" correctly) → tapped "▶ Turn-by-turn".
- What happened: an Android "Open with" chooser appeared (Maps / Motive Driver / Trucker Path / Uber / Zoom); while it was up, the button's TIMED CASCADE (modules/navigator.js, ttnLinks + attempt chain, `setTimeout(... attempt(links.waze, true), 2000)`) kept firing and opened https://waze.com/ul in Chrome — Waze's live-map directions WEB page with empty "Choose starting point / Choose destination" boxes. Owner: "Wth is this?"
- Root cause: the cascade's stillHere() check cannot see a system chooser (the page still counts as "here"), so fallback attempts keep firing after the user has already been handed off. A waze.com/ul link with no Waze app installed lands on the marketing/live-map web page — never acceptable as a navigation handoff.
- Fix direction (owner law: one tap, one unmistakable action): Turn-by-turn fires ONE handoff only (Android intent for Maps navigation; geo: as the single fallback when the intent scheme is unsupported) and the timed multi-attempt cascade is deleted. The waze web link must never be an automatic fallback. In-app Route → ▶ Drive remains the primary path.
- **NAVIGATION PLACES LIST IS ENDLESS (owner, Oct 16, 2026 16:44 MDT: "But why these endless endless lists").** The v102 search-first picker still renders ALL ~48 favorites as rows under the filter by default — search exists, but the wall of places is what he SEES first (his Oct 16 screen recording scrolls it). Fix: default view shows only RECENT destinations (the app already tracks visits + last-visit — cap ~6, most recent first); the full list appears ONLY as filter matches while typing. Nothing else about the picker changes (full names, one tap picks + arms Navigate).
- **TOO MANY BUTTONS BEFORE NAVIGATE (owner, Oct 16, 2026 16:45 MDT: "And why so many buttons before we get to navigate?").** The Navigator was built as a planning desk: mode buttons, Follow/Sound toggles, search, a duplicate "Your pits & job sites" dropdown, Route, Turn-by-turn, then Drive — the driver runs a gauntlet to start moving. Fix (owner law: one unmistakable primary action): (1) arriving with a destination armed (nav_pending from Navigation → Navigate, or a pick inside the Navigator) ROUTES IMMEDIATELY — no separate Route tap to reach Drive; (2) the one big primary button is **Drive**; Route remains available quietly for re-planning; (3) Truck/Car, Follow, Sound demote to a small quiet row (settings, not steps); (4) the Navigator's duplicate places dropdown is replaced by the same search-first recents list as Navigation (the three doors stay: favorite pick / address search / tap the map).
- **OWNER RULING — Oct 16, 2026 16:45 MDT: NO LEAVING THE APP.** "Besides turn by turn there shouldn't be any other applications to begin with — why is it offering to take it out of the application?" The v93 external maps handoff is SUPERSEDED by the v101 in-app Drive screen: turn-by-turn means OUR Drive screen. v103 therefore DELETES the "▶ Turn-by-turn" external handoff entirely (button, ttnLinks, the timed cascade, waze/geo/web links) instead of repairing the cascade. Navigation = pick destination → auto-route → ▶ Drive, all inside Meticulous. (Earlier fix direction for the cascade defect is replaced by this deletion.)

## v103 — Oct 16, 2026: IN-APP NAVIGATION ONLY (the handoff is DELETED) + recents picker + navigator simplification + in-app dispatch delete
All four fixes are the owner's Oct 16 evening rulings (see the field-defect entries above; the cascade-repair direction in the Waze entry is superseded by the NO-LEAVING-THE-APP ruling).
- FIX 1 — THE EXTERNAL HANDOFF IS GONE (modules/navigator.js): the "▶ Turn-by-turn" button, ttnLinks(), and the timed attempt cascade are DELETED — no waze.com/ul, no geo:, no intent://, no google.com/maps/dir anywhere in the navigator (production bytes verified: zero occurrences of each). The no-server presentation no longer offers another app either: with the server address cleared, the destination still arms and the note says routing needs the Navigator server — navigation never leaves Meticulous. The dispatch module's static "📍 Map" links are untouched (not a handoff path). What turn-by-turn means now: pick a destination → the route draws itself → ▶ Drive (the v101 drive screen, behavior untouched).
- FIX 2 — RECENTS-ONLY PICKER (modules/navigation.js, ONE shared component): new PICKER region exports pickerEntries/pickerRowHTML/pickerFind, used by BOTH the Navigation screen and the Navigator's places door. Default render (empty filter) = at most 6 most-recently-visited places (PICKER_RECENTS_MAX), most recent first; a place counts as visited via its own lastVisit OR a same-named entry in the recent-destinations log (built-ins carry no favorite record — a navigated built-in's recency comes from the log). The full combined list renders ONLY as filter matches while typing (no cap on matches). Full names, one tap picks + arms Navigate — unchanged. Empty states say so plainly ("Places you go to will show here — type above to find any place."). Stored favorites are never mutated by the picker (rows are copies).
- FIX 3 — NAVIGATOR SIMPLIFICATION (modules/navigator.js): (a) AUTO-ROUTE — an armed destination routes by itself: setDest arms a one-shot latch (autoKey/autoWanted); the route fires the moment destination + position + server are all present (a destination picked before the first GPS fix routes on that fix; End spends the latch so a finished drive never re-routes; a mode change re-routes a live plan, or re-arms an unrouted destination under the new mode). The Route button stays as the quiet re-planning affordance (primary only until a route is active, then ghost; Drive is the ONE big primary once a route exists). (b) The settings row is quiet: the active Truck/Car mode paints navy, the inactive ghost — the primary orange belongs to actions (Route/Drive) only; Follow/Sound were already ghost toggles. (c) The "Your pits & job sites" <select> is DELETED; the Destination card now carries the shared picker (filter #nv_pq + rows #nv_presults) — a pinned pick calls setDest on the exact pin; an address-only pick fills the search box and runs the same search path as an address-only nav_pending. The three doors stand: favorite pick / address search / tap-the-map pin. ## v104 — Oct 16, 2026: THAT'S TURN BY TURN (auto-enter Drive) + Drive pinned + real route errors + opening-balloon fix
Owner verdict on v103 (video, Oct 16, 2026 17:19 MDT): searched "12", tapped his 12th Street favorite, hit Navigate — the route COMPUTED (banner + route line + live distances counting down on the plan view) but the app stayed in the planning shell. "That's not turn by turn." The ▶ Drive button was not even in his viewport: the bottom sheet's recents list covered the lower area. A computed route the driver still has to hunt a button for is the button-gauntlet failure he already killed once. Four fixes, all in modules/navigator.js:
- FIX 1 — AUTO-ENTER DRIVE (go-now arms + already moving): `maybeAutoEnterDrive()` runs off doRoute's success path. It enters Drive by itself when (a) the route's destination was armed as a go-now hand-off — setDest's new `goNow` parameter: the nav_pending coordinates door passes it explicitly; the address door's intent is held as `S.pendGoNow` and consumed by the FIRST destination the search lands (a later parked pick in the same session does not inherit it); any nav_pending producer (Navigation→Navigate, dispatch-armed flows, the Butterfly favorite) inherits it — or (b) the truck is already moving: GPS speed ≥ 2 m/s, with speed now riding the module's ONE existing watchPosition stream into onPos (no new GPS consumer; last real value kept, like heading). Parked manual planning (in-Navigator pick, stationary) keeps the plan view. ONCE PER ARM: `driveAutoSpent` is set by the entry itself AND by any exitDrive (tap or arrival hand-back), and reset only by a NEW destination — an off-route or mode-change re-route for the same destination can never drag the driver back in. The v103 latch semantics are untouched: auto-route still fires once per new destination; End still spends the auto-route latch.
- FIX 2 — DRIVE CANNOT HIDE: `#nv_drive` moved out of the Destination card to the FIRST slot of `.nv-sheet`, above the destination box and the recents list — the sheet's top is always in the first viewport, fold or no fold. Reveal/hide + primary-slot swap logic unchanged (doRoute reveals + demotes Route to ghost; clearRoute hides + restores Route).
- FIX 3 — REAL ROUTE ERRORS: doRoute's catch no longer prints the v93-era guess "Check the Navigator server URL in Preferences → Advanced and your connection." — the text that blamed the settings through the whole Oct 16 dead-address/CORS hunt. The response's HTTP status is now captured and the note line names the actual failure class, one short line: fetch threw (no response at all) → "The route server didn't answer — check your connection and try again."; error status → "The route server answered with an error (HTTP <status>)."; OK answer with no legs → "No route could be built between here and there." The banner keeps the plain "Could not get a route." The no-server-configured state keeps its own honest line (that is a settings state, not a route failure). The 17:10 field-failure entry above is closed by this + the server-side CORS fix (shipped separately: Caddy now echoes ACAO for the native shell's https://localhost origin; the saved-URL suspect was cleared by the owner's 17:14 screenshot — his field already read https://2.31.47.19).
- FIX 4 — THE OPENING BALLOON, IDENTIFIED AND FIXED: the "large black oval" from the Oct 16 evening entries. Frame analysis of the owner's two videos (16:40 + 17:19) at 6 fps around the Navigator opening caught it in exactly one frame (~10.0 s in the 16:40 recording): a giant disc whose colours are the user-position circleMarker's own — fill #3f7de0 centre, #1F3764 ring. Mechanism: the seeded/stored fix painted the marker and animated the follow view while the shell was still settling, when Leaflet's size/zoom state was bogus; one settle later it snapped to the normal 8 px dot (visible in every later frame). It is NOT the drive tilt (the drive screen never opened in that recording), NOT a Leaflet tile/pane background, and nothing in the app draws an ellipse. Fix: position paints on the planning map now go through one writer (`paintPos`) gated on the existing 250 ms settle pass — the gate opens after invalidateSize and paints any waiting fix; the first PAINTED view jumps to zoom 15 with `animate: false` so no zoom transform can scale the overlay. The drive map's position marker likewise joins its 120 ms settle pass instead of painting at creation. Checked and left alone: .nv-map/.nv-shell backgrounds (light #e8edf5/#eef1f6 — the blank pre-tile map in the 17:19 video is light grey, not the disc), the banner overlay (a navy rounded rect, top-anchored, present in every frame), the app FABs (border-radius:50% but 56–60 px, bottom-right, app-wide — wrong size, wrong place). Observed in the same frames, NOT fixed here (different defect, not in this build's scope): the shell head's ← Back ghost button renders white-on-near-white (washed pill in the navy shell head) — its text is barely readable against the ghost background; logged for a styling pass.

## Oct 16, 2026 (v105) — P&L FLAWLESS + INVOICE EXACTLY AS SENT (the 18:55 build order, executed)

**JOB 1 — P&L.** The double-count family was LIVE in P&L: calc() income was a raw payFor sum over every stored entry, so a closed dispatch and its same-day tracker session stacked (the $601.75 / $608.87 defect). FIXED: P&L income is now priced by the SHARED per-day builder (invCycBuildRevenueLines — the same function the invoice prints from and Revenue totals with): one line per day, a confirmed completion beats the day's tracker entries. Proven against the owner's real October: P&L October income = $3,929.50 exactly (Oct 1–15 $3,610.50 + Oct 16 $319.00); the Oct 16 day contributes $319.00, never $601.75. Fuel receipts land as expenses automatically (Oct fills $234.32 + $193.80 + $199.91 = $628.03); totals equal the sum of their lines to the cent; the detail autos (brokerage 10% = $392.95) now price off the grouped income. Builder pricing aligned to the payFor rule everywhere it is used: an entry's recorded amount WINS, quantity × current rate is only the fallback for amount-less entries — a later rate edit or a Day Details amount edit can no longer be silently repriced by the invoice/Revenue/P&L. The builder also returns per-type totals (typeTotals side map) so P&L's income breakdown comes from the same grouped lines.
**THE HAUL CARD'S DOUBLE DUTY (owner, Oct 16 18:58 — cases pinned in tests):** dspCompletionPlan now emits the card's WAGE POSTING for the payer-side books, priced off its own rate: (1) driver completer — income = (foreman + travel) × rate, no posting (the entry IS his income); (2) office completer + identified driver — revenue = foreman ONLY × billing rate, wage posting = (foreman + travel) × the DRIVER's rate, superseding the v52 travel-only claim (no new claims are minted; P&L filters any legacy claim whose dispatch now carries a posting, and card-covered days are excluded from computed travel — the travel hour can never be paid twice); (3) owner-operator ('both') — the entry becomes CLIENT BILLING, foreman ONLY × billing rate, and a 'self' wage posting = (foreman + travel) × wage rate lands in his P&L: with billing $40 / wage $29 / foreman 10h / travel 1h → revenue $400.00, expense $319.00, card margin $81.00. Rates are Preferences fields (Client billing rate / My wage rate; blank = the plain rate). (4) NEVER TWICE: postings are keyed by dispatch (dspMergeCardWages, replace semantics, mirrored to pnl_card_wages) and completion revenue entries carry dspId — revenue.addEntry replaces a same-dispatch entry and now preserves the split fields (foremanHours / travelHours / travelInIncome / ticketNos) it used to drop. P&L shows the expense half as its own line: "↳ Haul-card pay (card pay 11h (foreman 10h + travel 1h) = $319.00)".
**JOB 2 — INVOICE.** The printed invoice now reproduces the sent #1151 natively: day lines in hour-sheet shape ("Oct 16: 11h") on screen and in print (invCycLineLabel from the winning day's hours); the closing note is GENERATED from the record's own facts (invCycDocNote) and matches the sent note byte-for-byte for Oct 1–15 ("Rate $29.00/hour, tax included (net rate $27.62/hour + 5% GST of $1.38/hour). Hours are the foreman ticket hours for each day plus 1 hour of travel per day worked. Oct 14: haul card #23177 (foreman 9.5h + 1h travel). Oct 15: haul card #21314 (foreman 10.5h + 1h travel). Total hours: 124.5."); page footer "Invoice N" added. Issue archives dayHours + cardDays + rate ON the record, so a reprint reproduces the document from the record alone. Bill-to carries the full address: new Preferences field "Contracting company address" (o.contractingAddr) composes the draft's Bill-to as "AI Trucking, 1506 16 St NE, Calgary, AB T2E 4T2" (an untouched default draft picks the address up; a hand-edited Bill-to is never rewritten). Oct 16–31 draft verified by harness: exactly one line (Oct 16: 11h), card #22729 in the note, totals $319.00 / GST-inside $15.19 / total due $319.00. Oct 14 audit blockers re-proven dead: (1) # field renders empty + Preferences sequence fills it (1152 next); (2) GST prefill forced from Preferences in tax-inclusive mode (default 5), jurisdiction banner only when country/province truly unset; (3) same-day dedup is the shared builder's (dispatch wins, tracker-only flagged, multi-tracker blocks); (4) issued-invoice save is the verified write + read-back (invoice-cycle suite).
- Tests: NEW tools/pnl-flawless-test.js 34/34 (October ground truth, the $601.75 poison, fuel $628.03, lines-sum identities, office books $290/$297/$594, owner-operator $400/$319/$81, never-twice merges + write-path pins); NEW tools/invoice-format-test.js 36/36 (#1151 byte-for-byte note + labels, Oct 16–31 draft, issue-record storage, blocker statics); role-package 161→178/178 (deliberate updates: 'both' leaves the pre-v52 parity loop for the two-books pins; office claim pins replaced by full-wage posting pins — owner order Oct 16 supersedes the v52 shapes); dispatch-close 73/74 (expectedEntry now includes the stamped dspId; the one fail is the standing index version pin, healed by this ship's pin move). Full suite: fail set identical to the v104 baseline (back-button 1, boss-firstrun 6, card-previews 5, dispatch-autostart clock-dependent set — proven identical on the pristine tree, manifest-export f1 + pin, manifest-toggles 2, park-place 1).

CLEANUP (Aisle 5): merge into ONE odometer card — one field, one photo button; when the entered value is below the stored master, the card itself asks "stored value looks wrong — save as a correction?" and writes the audit entry. Until merged: his live trap — a value typed into the UPDATE field silently cannot fix a corrupt (higher) master; only the Correct card's Save moves it down.
- **FUEL TREND INSANITY — corrupt-odometer downstream (his phone screenshot, Oct 16, 2026 19:29 MDT):** Fuel trend shows **4,484.5 km/L** for Oct 15 (Oct 14: 2.5) — the Oct 15 fill's distance delta was computed against the corrupt 519,326 stored reading, exploding the economy math. Correcting the master does NOT by itself repair historical fill-up odometer stamps — the trend needs the poisoned fill stamp(s) repaired or excluded. Also on the same screen: the fuel-up form shows "Power read failed" (brain key again). Cloud Maintenance header meanwhile reads master **168,759 km** (not 519,326) with "Due in 6,670 km" — the cloud master and the phone's artifacts disagree; the owner's typed correction to 169,166 (instructed Oct 16 19:28 MDT) is the unifying fix for the master; trend-stamp repair stays queued here.
- **P&L FUEL DISCREPANCY — DIAGNOSIS (Oct 16, 2026): NOT a calc bug; no ship (webVersion stays 105).** Code audit + NEW tools/pnl-fuel-gap-test.js 13/13 proves: P&L's ⛽ Fuel is a once-each prefix-sum over the SAME `fuel_fills` array the Fuel module lists (pnl.js calc: `inMonth(f.date, m)` = date-string prefix; single pass, `parseFloat(f.cost)`), and "Other (from Fuel module)" is the DISJOINT `fuel_expenses` store — a fill can never appear in both lines, nor twice in one. fuel.js is the ONLY writer of `fuel_fills` (verified single-array save; edits replace in place); NO flow — current or in git history — ever wrote a fill + expense mirror pair (the `FB.saveDoc('fuel', …)` docs are write-only: nothing ever loads that collection; sync mirrors whole keys, never appends entries). Date-window divergence is impossible in the upward direction: P&L buckets ONLY by the `f.date` prefix and the module list applies NO window at all (it renders every stored fill in stored/append order — NOT date order — and shows no month total anywhere; the $2,329.32 was a manual sum). A non-ISO date (e.g. '10/14/2026') is EXCLUDED by P&L, so format drift biases P&L DOWN, never up. Therefore the $443.80 is real entries in `fuel_fills` with '2026-10'-prefixed dates beyond the manually summed set. Candidate classes, in order: (a) October-dated fills sitting deep in the list — receipt-photo saves carry the RECEIPT's date and the list is append-ordered, so October rows scatter among neighbours; fills added on the browser copy live in the same array; (b) a duplicated fill (same date+cost twice — a manual sum tends to count a look-alike pair once; P&L counts both); (c) float-artifact cost strings (counted at full precision); (d) an October fill with cost but blank litres / unrecognized station (e.g. a verification entry) skipped as "not one of his". SETTLING CHECK (parent, on the signed-in account): on the SAME surface where P&L showed $2,773.12, open Fuel & Expense → Fill-ups and sum the $ column of EVERY row whose date starts 2026-10, top to bottom, duplicates included — it must total $2,773.12, and the extra rows summing to $443.80 will identify their own class by date/station. If that same-surface sum instead gives $2,329.32, the two reads hit different store states (phone-local vs cloud divergence — already documented on this account) and the comparison must be redone on one surface.

(3) **Revenue live stacking** (modules/revenue.js dayAgg): the running session's hours+value are never added on top of a confirmed dispatch/entry day, nor on a duplicate-flagged day — the 598.5-hour window dies here. - **v114 — PHONE/CLOUD CONVERGENCE (overnight program Stage 8, Oct 16, 2026, shipped):** the Oct 16 divergence mechanism is closed in js/cloudsync.js's pull. Root cause: a fresh local copy materializes DEFAULT values stamped newer than the cloud's real values, so whole-key "latest wins" kept the defaults and the flush pushed them up over the cloud (his phone: Revenue $0, auto-start OFF/6 AM, default Preferences). Two rules now run before the whole-key merge: (1) **'onboarding' merges FIELD BY FIELD on every pull** — a SET field beats an UNSET one on the other copy; fields set on both sides keep the whole-key timestamp winner; if the merged union holds anything the cloud lacks, the key stays dirty so the union pushes UP. Neither copy can silently lose a set value again — the phone's Alberta survives the cloud's blank, and the cloud's company details survive the phone's blanks. (2) **First pull for a uid**: a local value that is EMPTY ([], {}, '', null) never beats a non-empty cloud value, whatever the timestamps say. The guard stands: where both sides genuinely hold set values, the newer timestamp still wins — pinned both directions. Tests: NEW tools/cloudsync-merge-test.js **14/14** driving the real engine (first-pull union + push-up, newer-local wins, newer-cloud wins). webVersion 113 → 114. **This closes the overnight program's eight stages (v107–v114).** Remaining parent items: the ai-service Origin allowlist for the phone WebView + redeploy (Stage 5 server half), the live-UI acceptance drive, and confirming the odometer master = 169,166 on the owner's account.

- **v115 — POWER READ: TOLERANT ODOMETER EXTRACTION (Oct 16, 2026, shipped):** the dash-read parse brittleness from the v114 acceptance drive is fixed at both ends. **Root cause, traced:** in js/powerread.js the model's reply reached `fields.value` through ONE door — `parseModelJson` (strict JSON, else a `{…}` slice) feeding `cleanInt(j.value)`. A prose reply ("The odometer reads 169,166 km") has no braces → null → the verbatim **"⚡ Power read could not understand the answer"**; `{"value": 169,166}` (a comma inside an unquoted number) is invalid JSON even after slicing → same death; a bare `169166` parses to a JSON *number* → `mapFields` discarded non-objects → read "succeeded" empty. The fuel flow survived the same evening because reader 'text' hands prose to a tolerant local parser downstream — the odometer path had no tolerant fallback at all. **App fix (js/powerread.js):** new `PowerRead._extractOdometer` recovers the reading from ANY reply text — thousands separators stripped, space-split digits glued, every number a candidate; candidates are 4–7 digit values in the plausible truck span **1,000–2,000,000 km**; trip-meter numbers lose (labelled "trip", or carrying a decimal while an integer stands); labelled/total/"km"-suffixed figures win; against a stored master the candidate NEAREST it (inside the existing 1,000 km jump window) wins, else the largest dash-like figure; no candidate → null → the honest failure stands. `mapFields` keeps the strict door first (object `value`, then a bare integer JSON number), then falls back to the extractor over the raw reply; both call routes (proxy + direct key) pass the raw text through and only report "could not understand" when NOTHING was recovered. Every guard stands: the extracted value is still only a candidate — escalate()'s two-sided plausibility gate judges it (a prose 45,100 against his 169,166 master is refused as 'below', suite-pinned), the confirm-first chip/field flow is untouched, and nothing persists without his tap/save. **Server fix (repo copy ONLY — `~/workspace/truck-nav/deploy/ai/server.js`, parent deploys):** the odometer reader's instruction now demands a bare number — "Reply with only the odometer number, no other text: the integer total kilometres with no commas, spaces or labels… If you truly cannot read it, reply with only: 0" (0 sits below the app's 1,000 km floor, so it maps to an honest no-reading); the app-side promptFor mirrors it for the direct-key route; sticker + text prompts unchanged. Tests: NEW tools/power-read-odo-extract-test.js **38/38** (the pinned failure shapes: prose with comma, bare number, "ODO: 169166 KM", invalid-JSON braces, trip+odometer in both decimal and integer-trip forms, the 0 sentinel, garbage → honest error, implausible prose still gated, confirm-first write set). Full suite vs the v114 baseline: fail set IDENTICAL — the 3 standing rows only (background-day E8 stale 104 assert, chatbot H2 stale 104 assert, manifest-export f1 seconds fixture) — plus the new suite green; power-read 83/83, power-read-integration 77/77, odo suites all green. webVersion 114 → 115, index pins moved (19 × ?v=115), manifest regenerated (44 files; only js/powerread.js + index.html hashes moved). **REMAINING (parent):** deploy the server.js prompt change to the Hetzner box (rebuild meticulous-ai — the file is image-baked), then re-drive the live acceptance: the owner's dash photo through the merged odometer card on his signed-in app. Per the USER-PROOF STANDARD, Power Read (odometer) stays NOT-WORKING until his own in-app read lands.

It's supposed to ask in the preferences."):** the v112 dashboard card is DELETED — the #parkHome card and wireParkHome are gone from index.html/js/app.js, and the home face carries the tracker + module cards only. Location may be off for Meticulous, or this build has no location. Nothing was set.") can now only ever appear inside that Preferences card. New ids pf_park_note/pf_park_set/pf_park_nn/pf_park_forget (the retired v74 ids stay retired — role-package pins them absent). **(B) REVENUE CHIPS WRAP (21:41, his screenshot: "Custom" half off the right edge):** the pay-period chips row is now `.rvchips` — flex-wrap with 3 chips per row at phone width (flex-basis 28%, gap 8px, min-width 0), selected styling and 44px-class tap targets untouched; money-truth pins the fit arithmetically at 360px (row 280px: 3 chips + 2 gaps fit, a 4th cannot → wraps, nothing crosses the edge, no overflow-x anywhere). **(C) THE DAY DETAIL + FLAT LIST TELL THE HEADER'S TRUTH (21:42 screenshot + 21:50 follow-up):** Thu Oct 15's detail header was right ($333.50 / 11.5h) while its 📒 Entries listed FOUR money rows summing $667.47 — sheet import + dispatch completion + two 0.01h tracker drips, each dressed as money. dayDetailData now partitions the day's records through the SAME shared builder ladder the header prices from: the entries list is exactly the winning source's records (Oct 15 → the dispatch record alone, $333.50, sums matching the header to the cent and the hour); every other record returns as EVIDENCE and renders in a new collapsed **"🗂 Also recorded — not counted in this day's totals"** card with NO dollar amounts (Edit/Delete kept, so the duplicate banner's "fix it below" stays actionable; a duplicate day starts the card open). Tracker drips (auto entries under 0.1 of the pay unit) are never money entries. **The flat 📒 Entries list got the same cure** (21:50 screenshot: TODAY led with "$282.85 · Auto from tracker" over the real "$319.00 · Dispatch … Ticket #22729"): new entryMoneyFlags() applies the ladder per day — the flat list renders money entries only, grouped newest day first, so a day's priced record always leads; a tracker entry is the day's entry ONLY when it is the day's priced source; non-money records live in the day's detail, one tap from its money row. With no builder present the legacy all-entries behavior stands (the same fallback dayAgg makes). **(D) THE GRAPH'S TWO-TAP, RESTORED (21:47: the pattern he loved in the old design):** chart bars AND Month-view day rows are two-tap now — first tap SELECTS (the bar paints navy / the row highlights) and a preview card shows the day's label, hours, earnings and loads, priced by dayAgg + the shared dayLoads so the preview can never disagree with the detail; second tap on the SAME day opens its detail; a tap on a different day just moves the selection. Pure decision fn graphTap; month/year GROUPINGS note: Revenue has no by-month/by-year grouping rows today — the two surfaces that offer period entries (bars, Month day rows) both carry the pattern. **(E) BACK IS A STAIRCASE, NOT AN EXIT (21:44, his screen recording: Revenue → Oct 15 detail → Back dumped him on the dashboard; re-entering Revenue landed back on the stale detail):** two shell rules in js/app.js — **App.moduleBack()**: every back press (header arrow AND Android system back, both rewired) first offers the open module its sub-view via mod.back() (true = handled, stay); only a module on its main view exits. **App.openModule()** calls **mod.resetView()** before EVERY fresh render, so entering a module always lands on its main view. Implemented per module after audit: Revenue (day detail + open edit unwind; resetView clears them), Fuel (an open fill-up edit cancels first), Invoice (the non-home mode returns to the home mode — Invoice, or Pay Stub for payroll), Navigator (Back while driving exits Drive to the plan view via the S.backFn hook render installs; its own ← Back button follows the same rule; fresh entry needed nothing — render rebuilds S), Preferences (Back on the Advanced tab returns to Settings), Dispatch (back disarms an armed delete confirm; **deliberately NO resetView** — the dispatch form draft, including an in-progress edit, is saved user work under the v48 "never lose a dispatch form" law, not a navigation sub-view; restoring it on entry is the designed behavior, distinct from Revenue's stale pure-view pointer). Maintenance and P&L audited: single scroll views, no sub-view state — nothing to unwind. Tests: money-truth 36 → **64/64** (Oct 15 four-record fixture: one money entry, sums exactly the header, drips flagged in evidence; Oct 16 flat-list flags: dispatch money / tracker not; tracker-only day: session is the entry; graphTap state machine ×3 + preview/statics); back-button 54 → **71/71** (moduleBack extracted and driven through the shell's own expression: detail→main→dashboard; resetView contract; per-module contract statics incl. the Dispatch draft-law pin); day-detail **33/33** (evidence partition present + empty under the no-builder fallback); records-open **87/87** (two v75 single-tap statics deliberately updated to the two-tap contract); revenue-grouped **29/29** (the "lists both raw entries" pin updated in shape, not intent: 1 money + 1 evidence); park-place **60/60**; role-package **177/177**; account-login **46/46** (a first-cut of the park painter crashed vm suites with no global document — it now guards and bails quietly). - **v117 — THE DISPATCH EDIT, WHOLE (Oct 16, 2026, shipped; owner screenshot + words, 21:53 MDT: "I get it that editing isn't working! Why is it showing me on 2 cards?"):** the Dispatch edit experience had three defects on one screen. **(1) ONE RECORD, ONE PRESENCE:** while the "✏️ Edit dispatch" form was open, the History list below still rendered the SAME dispatch as its own card (with the v75 "You're editing this one" banner + "✏️ Editing ↑" button). Now the History render SKIPS the record under edit (histShown) — the open form IS the record's one presence; the card returns by itself when the edit closes (Save changes / Cancel edit clear the edit and re-render), and an all-editing history reads "No other dispatches yet — the one you are editing is in the form above." The v75 marker is superseded by the owner's rule, pins moved with notes in dispatch-edit (j) and records-open (d). **(2) THE FORM OPENS FULL:** startEdit pre-filled ONLY from the record's top-level fields — but the Oct 16 dispatch's facts live on its TICKET (the haul card read), so Material/Ticket # opened blank, Amount showed 0.00, Loads reset to the bare default 1. The pre-fill is now one pure function, **dspEditFieldsFor(record)**: a set top-level field always wins; a blank one is promoted from the tickets — ticket # from the first card/ticket number ("22729"), material from the first ticket that carries one, Loads from the tickets' loads sum (6, never the bare default when a recorded value exists; ticket-promoted loads ride loadsSet=true, the same trust dspBuildClosed gives a ticket sum); a zero amount opens blank (placeholder), not a fake 0.00. Round-trip pinned: edit → Save changes → reopen shows material/ticket #/loads exactly. **(3) GIBBERISH NEVER PRINTS:** the stored read summary under his ticket still printed **"Read: Job site ONBNERREEEEARAL HH HHE"** — the v108 gate cleaned the form fields but not the summary, and stored summaries render verbatim forever. The gate now covers the summary at BOTH ends: TicketOCR.describeRead demotes any "found" whose free-text value is gibberish to an honest missing at composition, and a new **dspCleanDocRead** scrubs STORED summaries at render (both render sites — the form's ticket list and the close-with-ticket confirm): a dirty entry never prints; its label joins the "Couldn't find" list instead. Clean reports pass through byte-identical and the stored object is never mutated. - **v118 — THE OVERNIGHT SOAK, ALL ELEVEN ITEMS (Oct 16, 2026, shipped; source: the 3-circuit user-soak on the live app with the owner's real data, Oct 16 ~21:36–21:53 MDT, plus two owner orders at 21:56 and 22:02 MDT):** **(1) SAFETY STICKER DATE REVERTED — mechanism found:** the expiry lives in TWO stores (onboarding.safetyExpiry + mt_annualDate) with a ONE-WAY mirror — every Preferences save copied the form's value over mt_annualDate. His 2027-05-31 (set and verified "Due in 227 days" 19:44) was stomped back to 2027-05-01 by the later Preferences save that evening, whose form still held the older May-1 value. Fix: a winner rule (**pfSafetyWinner** — an untouched form value NEVER overwrites a different stored annualDate; the stored one is adopted; a value actually edited in the form still wins), the render captures what the field opened with, and the Maintenance save now mirrors INTO onboarding too, so neither store can resurrect a stale date. The month/year steppers still compose the LAST day of the punched month (May 2027 → 2027-05-31). **DATA NOTE: the stored value on his account still reads 2027-05-01 until it is re-set once (cloud-side or one stepper tap) — the code now guarantees it can never revert again; flagged for the parent to restore 2027-05-31 on the account.** **(2) MANIFEST END TIME on real data:** v107's competition ran only for the DERIVED card finish; a ticket carrying a READ finishTime (his real card's "6:00 PM") short-circuited it outright — the fixture's tickets carried none, so it passed while his real day printed 6:00 PM. The card's finish (read or derived, the later of the two — the same fact two ways) now competes with the session's end, later wins: Oct 16 → 7:00 PM (tracker), Oct 15 stays 6:30 PM (card). The close-tap priority when NO card end exists is untouched (a regression there was caught by manifest-times and fixed before shipping). **(3) RECENTS PASTED COORDINATES:** a place saved from a pin can carry its own coordinates AS its name; every surface now resolves through **displayPlaceName** (navigation.js, exported): a real name wins, a coordinates-name borrows the name of the saved place at the same pin (favorites + built-ins, both tabs), an unnamed pin reads "Dropped pin" — picker rows, both pickers' taps, "Go again", the detail panel, recordNav's recents log and the Navigator's destination label all resolve; raw coordinates never become the visible query. **(4) P&L TOTAL MILES:** was max−min over the month's fill stamps, which trusted the poisoned 519,326 stamp as the max: 519,326 − 168,510 = the soak's exact 350,816. Now the SUM of plausible odometer legs between consecutive readings (forward, ≤ 50,000 km — the v109 leg rule); the poisoned stamp's legs both fail, the honest legs remain (his October: 249 km on the pinned fixture shape; clean data still telescopes to last − first). Manual override still wins. **(5) POWER READ RETRY:** ONE automatic retry inside PowerRead.read on transient failure only (network class, or HTTP 5xx from either route — status now rides the failure results); quota/bad-key/no-model/unparseable are never retried; if the retry also fails transiently the copy says so honestly ("failed twice…"). **(6) INVOICE BILL-TO CASING:** no transform existed in code — a draft frozen with "Ai trucking" never matched the v105 EXACT-equality heal, so the wrong casing printed forever. **invBillToHeal** now treats any casing of the saved company (with or without the saved address) as untouched and replaces it with the saved value verbatim ("AI Trucking, 1506 16 St NE, Calgary, AB T2E 4T2"); a genuinely different hand-typed Bill-to is never rewritten. **(7) FUEL FORM FLOAT JUNK:** a read that derives litres as cost ÷ price left 88.10600280761719 / 199.91000366210935 in the fields; both fill sites (local + power read) and the edit prefill now round through fuRoundL/fuRoundC (3dp litres, 2dp money) — the fields hold exactly what the read line says, and saves inherit the clean values. **(8) DASHBOARD REVENUE PREVIEW:** status() mirrored the module's selected window ("Last 16d: $319" one hour, "Last 30d: $7308" the next); it is now a FIXED "Last 30d" figure summed from dayAgg over the trailing 30 days, independent of rev_period (pinned under 7d and custom selections). **(9) REVENUE LOADS BLANK:** monthSummary never computed loads and the Month view had no Loads card at all; the month's loads now ride the shared dayLoads ladder and the card renders the number (0 shows as 0, never a blank). **(10) FUEL ENTRY DETAILS PAGE (owner spec 21:56):** a Fill-ups row tap now opens a dedicated read-only DETAILS view — date/time, station, litres (3dp), total cost, price per litre, odometer, fuel type and note when stored, and the entry's receipt PHOTO (clean "No photo for this entry" state when none) — with Edit (reuses the existing edit flow, pre-filled) and Delete (two-tap arm, verified save) as quiet secondaries; Back returns to the Fill-ups list (the Fuel back contract gained the details layer; resetView clears it). **(11) DASHBOARD MANIFEST PREVIEW (owner order 22:02):** the card summed the raw session span ("9 h 45 m worked" for Oct 16); it now quotes the shared BUILDER's day when a priced record speaks for today ("11 h 0 m worked" — the same number the Manifest shows), and keeps the session span only for tracker-only days, where the session IS the truth. The other dashboard previews were audited, not assumed: P&L and Invoice already quote priced records, Fuel's last-fill is a stored fact — all three verified against their code paths. - **v121 — THE SIDEWAYS PAN, KILLED AT THE ROOT (Oct 17, 2026, shipped; owner evidence: his Oct 16 21:56 MDT screen recording of the Fuel screen, re-sent Oct 17 07:01 MDT — "Why is this acting like a website? Why is the entire panel not straight?"):** while scrolling, the whole panel dragged sideways and sat crooked. Root cause, measured in a real engine (headless Chrome, the owner's own data shapes, every screen at 360px and 412px): the page carried NO horizontal lock anywhere, and one mechanism sized it wide — flex/grid children whose floor is their content's min-content summing past the screen. On the v120 tree at 360px the document measured 433px on the DASHBOARD (module grid: the nowrap card subtitles give the two columns a ~449px hard minimum), 422px on P&L (the three stat cards), 408px on INVOICE (the period-to date input's intrinsic width), 376px on DISPATCH (the start-time input), 370px on REVENUE (a stat card) — and once the document is wide, the pan is there to be dragged on any screen; it grows worse with handset text size, which inflates every floor (his Fuel form rows are the same construction). THE FIX, CSS-only, three layers: (1) THE LOCK — html, body and #app now carry overflow-x (clip where supported — it creates no scroll container, so the sticky topbar and module heads keep sticking; hidden as the fallback line for older WebViews); the page can never be dragged sideways again, whatever any future element does. (2) THE GUARDS — flex/grid children (.row, .kv, .modgrid, .optgrid, .hero-btns) and form controls (input/select/textarea) get min-width:0, so a row shrinks/wraps inside its own box instead of sizing the page. (3) THE STRINGS — list-row text and .kv values wrap anywhere; images cap at their container. No module code, no data, no other behavior touched. "Starts from now" survives ONLY when no plan exists (auto-start off / wrong day / plan already fired); a manual preset start still wins the line. `autostartTodayInfo` also reports `fired` + `dispatchName` now. - **v125 — DRIVE, CALMED + STEADIED (Oct 17, 2026, shipped; local 2f862f7 + f20566f → remote 128f89b + 6301760 via gh-push-api.py; live webVersion 125, 43/43 manifest hashes verified against live bytes): the owner's own evidence — his 08:56 MDT screen recording on Deerfoot Trail SE, his 09:01 landscape screenshot, and his 09:04 words verbatim: "The issue is its flickering! And yes the colours are no good! Direction line has to be apparent.. And different colour (bold) and should retract as travelled upon!"** All measurements below are from a real engine (headless Chrome, real Leaflet, the LIVE app, the REAL route server) driving a real Deerfoot-area route (Bow Bottom → Deerfoot → exit 245 → Maple Ridge) through BOTH a keep/stay step and an exit step, at 390×844 and 844×390, before and after. **(1) FLICKER — the headline defect.** Mechanism, measured on v124: Leaflet's tile fade-in repaints every fresh tile from opacity 0 on every recenter, and a drive recenters every GPS tick — across 217 samples at 250 ms, **62% of samples had tiles mid-render** (max 78 at once), with 131–187 tile nodes created over ~1 minute of driving; the v124 saturate/contrast CSS filter additionally forced a full tile repaint on every composite. THE FIX: the drive map is created with `fadeAnimation:false` (tiles land whole), the tile layer keeps a deeper buffer (`keepBuffer:4`), and the filter is gone with the basemap change (below). The 3D tilt STAYS (52°) — post-fix, mid-fade tiles measure **0 across all 247 samples** (max 0, zero samples >0) in both orientations; what remains in the test rig is tiles still in flight over the test network, which is loading, not flashing. **(2) HOT PINK — what it actually was.** The drive tile source (verified in code and by computed style in the engine: `contrast(1.16) saturate(1.42)`, 200+ tile requests to tile.openstreetmap.org) is OSM Carto, which colours motorways **salmon pink natively**; v124's "stronger lift" pushed that salmon to the recording's neon pink, and the wide pink dashed bands were the same rendering on the parallel carriageways. THE FIX: the drive basemap is now **Esri World Street Map** (`driveTileSpec`, keyless with attribution) drawn with **NO filter** — cream land, white/grey-cased roads, arterials a pale tan. First pick was CARTO Voyager, but CARTO's public basemap CDN now answers every tile with an "API KEY REQUIRED" placeholder (verified against the live CDN + its Fastly mirror mid-build; the first v125 soak rendered the watermark — caught, corrected, re-shipped in f20566f before this entry was written). Pixel audit (sharp) of the map area: hot-pink-signature pixels **8.82% in his recording frame → 0.03%** in the shipped render on Deerfoot itself. The planning map is UNCHANGED (OSM + its gentler 1.08/1.18 lift). **(3) THE DIRECTION LINE (owner spec, exact).** Core was orange #e67e00 — one shade from the basemap's own road colours. Now a **deep blue #1a56db, 12px under the 17px white casing**, and it **RETRACTS as travelled**: new pure `routeRemainder(line, pos)` returns the untravelled geometry from the truck's projected position; driveTick redraws casing+core from it (≥10 m of progress or a segment change). Pinned pure (at origin = whole line; past midpoint the drawn start sits AT the truck; collapses drawable at the destination; monotone shrink) and proven in the engine: at both step captures, in both orientations, the drawn path's first point equals the position puck's centre **exactly** (SVG coords), and the screenshots show the line beginning at the truck with only the road ahead drawn. **(4) THE BAR — measured honestly.** On the LIVE v124 build the ETA **did** populate and End drive rendered (96 px) at both step types and both orientations — his recording's total loss (only the Sound pill, the PRE-v124 0 px signature pixel-for-pixel) does **not reproduce on v124 code in any tested state**: the copy running on his phone during that drive was still the pre-v124 Drive build (a stale OTA copy — the same story v124 recorded for his pre-v107 tilt). What v124 genuinely got wrong, measured: (a) the ETA column lost the flex fight to the buttons and landed at **105 px** in portrait, its sub-line truncated to "Arrive · 4 min · …"; (b) the shell **chat FAB sat ON the bar** — elementFromPoint at End drive's centre returned the FAB in portrait, and in landscape the FAB covered the Sound button; (c) the ETA stack was blank until the first successful tick. THE FIX (hardened so no build state can present that bar): the ETA column takes ALL leftover space (`flex:1 1 0%`), the sub-line **wraps to two lines** instead of truncating (post-fix portrait: the full "Arrive · 4 min · 5.5 km left · to Maple Ridge" readable, scroll measures show zero clipping), the stack renders a **visible placeholder from the moment Drive opens** ("--:--" / "Getting your arrival time…", replaced by the live first tick), Sound tightens a step, and **the chat FAB is hidden while Drive is open** (`body.mt-driving`, set in startDrive / cleared in exitDrive — the one exit funnel). Post-fix hit-tests: End drive's centre = `nvd_exit` in both orientations; the mic FAB still floats over the map only, never the bar. **(5) LANDSCAPE.** On v124 the banner measured **124 of 390 px (32%)** and the tilt plane's corner fell short, leaving the dark sky-gradient **wedge** his screenshot shows top-right. THE FIX: under `(orientation:landscape) and (max-height:500px)` the banner goes compact and horizontal (distance left, instruction + road stacked beside it, 30px/16px type) — post-fix banner height **52 px (13%)**; the tilt plane grows 300% → **340%** and the dark wedge is gone (at extreme corners a pale sliver of the basemap's own background tone can still show — it reads as map, not as a hole); the bar keeps ALL contents (in landscape the sub fits one full line, 592 px). Tests: navigator-test **167/167** (7 new pure pins: driveTileSpec + routeRemainder), navigator-screen **32/32** (v124 pins for the drive lift / 300% plane / ellipsized sub / 11px orange core moved DELIBERATELY with the recording cited in-suite; new pins: Esri spec, no drive-tile filter, fadeAnimation off + keepBuffer, FAB hide, landscape compaction, ETA placeholder, blue retracting line). FULL SUITE: fail set EXACTLY the 3 standing baseline rows (background-day clock · chatbot H2 stale-104, now reading "got 125" · manifest-export f1 seconds fixture).
